	% -----
	\subsubsection{Revelation 9} % *REVELATIONNT-9 
	% -----
		\label{REVELATIONNT-9}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 9:1} % *REVELATIONNT-9-1 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-9-1}

				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					See \hyperref[DC77-13]{DC 77:13}.
					}
			AND the fifth angel sounded, and I saw a star fall from heaven unto the earth: and to him was given the key of the bottomless pit.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ ὁ πέμπτος ἄγγελος ἐσάλπισεν· καὶ εἶδον ἀστέρα ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ πεπτωκότα εἰς τὴν γῆν, καὶ ἐδόθη αὐτῷ ἡ κλεὶς τοῦ φρέατος τῆς ἀβύσσου·}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 9:2} % *REVELATIONNT-9-2 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-9-2}

			And he opened the bottomless pit; and there arose a smoke out of the pit, as the smoke of a great furnace; and the sun and the air were darkened by reason of the smoke of the pit.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἤνοιξεν τὸ φρέαρ τῆς ἀβύσσου, καὶ ἀνέβη καπνὸς ἐκ τοῦ φρέατος ὡς καπνὸς καμίνου μεγάλης, καὶ ἐσκοτώθη ὁ ἥλιος καὶ ὁ ἀὴρ ἐκ τοῦ καπνοῦ τοῦ φρέατος.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 9:3} % *REVELATIONNT-9-3 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-9-3}

			And there came out of the smoke locusts upon the earth: and unto them was given power, as the scorpions of the earth have power.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἐκ τοῦ καπνοῦ ἐξῆλθον ἀκρίδες εἰς τὴν γῆν, καὶ ἐδόθη αὐταῖς ἐξουσία ὡς ἔχουσιν ἐξουσίαν οἱ σκορπίοι τῆς γῆς.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 9:4} % *REVELATIONNT-9-4 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-9-4}

			And it was commanded them that they should not hurt the grass of the earth, neither any green thing, neither any tree; but only those men which have not the seal of God in their foreheads.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἐρρέθη αὐταῖς ἵνα μὴ ἀδικήσουσιν τὸν χόρτον τῆς γῆς οὐδὲ πᾶν χλωρὸν οὐδὲ πᾶν δένδρον, εἰ μὴ τοὺς ἀνθρώπους οἵτινες οὐκ ἔχουσι τὴν σφραγῖδα τοῦ θεοῦ ἐπὶ τῶν μετώπων.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 9:5} % *REVELATIONNT-9-5 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-9-5}

			And to them it was given that they should not kill them, but that they should be tormented five months: and their torment was as the torment of a scorpion, when he striketh a man.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἐδόθη αὐτοῖς ἵνα μὴ ἀποκτείνωσιν αὐτούς, ἀλλ᾽ ἵνα βασανισθήσονται μῆνας πέντε· καὶ ὁ βασανισμὸς αὐτῶν ὡς βασανισμὸς σκορπίου, ὅταν παίσῃ ἄνθρωπον.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 9:6} % *REVELATIONNT-9-6 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-9-6}

			And in those days shall men seek death, and shall not find it; and shall desire to die, and death shall flee from them.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἐν ταῖς ἡμέραις ἐκείναις ζητήσουσιν οἱ ἄνθρωποι τὸν θάνατον καὶ οὐ μὴ εὑρήσουσιν αὐτόν, καὶ ἐπιθυμήσουσιν ἀποθανεῖν καὶ φεύγει ὁ θάνατος ἀπ᾽ αὐτῶν.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 9:7} % *REVELATIONNT-9-7 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-9-7}

			And the shapes of the locusts were like unto horses prepared unto battle; and on their heads were as it were crowns like gold, and their faces were as the faces of men.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ τὰ ὁμοιώματα τῶν ἀκρίδων ὅμοια ἵπποις ἡτοιμασμένοις εἰς πόλεμον, καὶ ἐπὶ τὰς κεφαλὰς αὐτῶν ὡς στέφανοι ὅμοιοι χρυσῷ, καὶ τὰ πρόσωπα αὐτῶν ὡς πρόσωπα ἀνθρώπων,}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 9:8} % *REVELATIONNT-9-8 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-9-8}

			And they had hair as the hair of women, and their teeth were as the teeth of lions.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ εἶχον τρίχας ὡς τρίχας γυναικῶν, καὶ οἱ ὀδόντες αὐτῶν ὡς λεόντων ἦσαν,}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 9:9} % *REVELATIONNT-9-9 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-9-9}

			And they had breastplates, as it were breastplates of iron; and the sound of their wings was as the sound of chariots of many horses running to battle.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ εἶχον θώρακας ὡς θώρακας σιδηροῦς, καὶ ἡ φωνὴ τῶν πτερύγων αὐτῶν ὡς φωνὴ ἁρμάτων ἵππων πολλῶν τρεχόντων εἰς πόλεμον·}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 9:10} % *REVELATIONNT-9-10 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-9-10}

			And they had tails like unto scorpions, and there were stings in their tails: and their power was to hurt men five months.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἔχουσιν οὐρὰς ὁμοίας σκορπίοις καὶ κέντρα, καὶ ἐν ταῖς οὐραῖς αὐτῶν ἡ ἐξουσία αὐτῶν ἀδικῆσαι τοὺς ἀνθρώπους μῆνας πέντε.}

		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-9-11}

			And they had a king over them, which is the angel of the bottomless pit, whose name in the Hebrew tongue is Abaddon, but in the Greek tongue hath his name Apollyon.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἔχουσιν ἐπ᾽ αὐτῶν βασιλέα τὸν ἄγγελον τῆς ἀβύσσου· ὄνομα αὐτῷ Ἑβραϊστὶ Ἀβαδδών καὶ ἐν τῇ Ἑλληνικῇ ὄνομα ἔχει Ἀπολλύων.}

		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-9-14}

			Saying to the sixth angel which had the trumpet, Loose the four angels which are bound in the great river Euphrates.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{λέγοντα τῷ ἕκτῳ ἀγγέλῳ, ὁ ἔχων τὴν σάλπιγγα· Λῦσον τοὺς τέσσαρας ἀγγέλους τοὺς δεδεμένους ἐπὶ τῷ ποταμῷ τῷ μεγάλῳ Εὐφράτῃ.}

		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-9-15}

			And the four angels were loosed, which were prepared for an hour, and a day, and a month, and a year, for to slay the third part of men.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἐλύθησαν οἱ τέσσαρες ἄγγελοι οἱ ἡτοιμασμένοι εἰς τὴν ὥραν καὶ ἡμέραν καὶ μῆνα καὶ ἐνιαυτόν, ἵνα ἀποκτείνωσιν τὸ τρίτον τῶν ἀνθρώπων.}

		% ---

			And the number of the army of the horsemen were two hundred thousand thousand: and I heard the number of them.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ὁ ἀριθμὸς τῶν στρατευμάτων τοῦ ἱππικοῦ δισμυριάδες μυριάδων· ἤκουσα τὸν ἀριθμὸν αὐτῶν.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 9:17} % *REVELATIONNT-9-17 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-9-17}

			And thus I saw the horses in the vision, and them that sat on them, having breastplates of fire, and of jacinth, and brimstone: and the heads of the horses were as the heads of lions; and out of their mouths issued fire and smoke and brimstone.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ οὕτως εἶδον τοὺς ἵππους ἐν τῇ ὁράσει καὶ τοὺς καθημένους ἐπ᾽ αὐτῶν, ἔχοντας θώρακας πυρίνους καὶ ὑακινθίνους καὶ θειώδεις· καὶ αἱ κεφαλαὶ τῶν ἵππων ὡς κεφαλαὶ λεόντων, καὶ ἐκ τῶν στομάτων αὐτῶν ἐκπορεύεται πῦρ καὶ καπνὸς καὶ θεῖον.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]

					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 9:18} % *REVELATIONNT-9-18 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-9-18}

			By these three was the third part of men killed, by the fire, and by the smoke, and by the brimstone, which issued out of their mouths.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀπὸ τῶν τριῶν πληγῶν τούτων ἀπεκτάνθησαν τὸ τρίτον τῶν ἀνθρώπων, ἐκ τοῦ πυρὸς καὶ τοῦ καπνοῦ καὶ τοῦ θείου τοῦ ἐκπορευομένου ἐκ τῶν στομάτων αὐτῶν.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 9:19} % *REVELATIONNT-9-19 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-9-19}

			For their power is in their mouth, and in their tails: for their tails were like unto serpents, and had heads, and with them they do hurt.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἡ γὰρ ἐξουσία τῶν ἵππων ἐν τῷ στόματι αὐτῶν ἐστιν καὶ ἐν ταῖς οὐραῖς αὐτῶν· αἱ γὰρ οὐραὶ αὐτῶν ὅμοιαι ὄφεσιν, ἔχουσαι κεφαλάς, καὶ ἐν αὐταῖς ἀδικοῦσιν.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]

			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 9:20} % *REVELATIONNT-9-20 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-9-20}

			And the rest of the men which were not killed by these plagues yet repented not of the works of their hands, that they should not worship devils, and idols of gold, and silver, and brass, and stone, and of wood: which neither can see, nor hear, nor walk:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ οἱ λοιποὶ τῶν ἀνθρώπων, οἳ οὐκ ἀπεκτάνθησαν ἐν ταῖς πληγαῖς ταύταις, οὐδὲ μετενόησαν ἐκ τῶν ἔργων τῶν χειρῶν αὐτῶν, ἵνα μὴ προσκυνήσουσιν τὰ δαιμόνια καὶ τὰ εἴδωλα τὰ χρυσᾶ καὶ τὰ ἀργυρᾶ καὶ τὰ χαλκᾶ καὶ τὰ λίθινα καὶ τὰ ξύλινα, ἃ οὔτε βλέπειν δύνανται οὔτε ἀκούειν οὔτε περιπατεῖν,}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 9:21} % *REVELATIONNT-9-21 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-9-21}

			Neither repented they of their murders, nor of their sorceries, nor of their fornication, nor of their thefts.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ οὐ μετενόησαν ἐκ τῶν φόνων αὐτῶν οὔτε ἐκ τῶν φαρμάκων αὐτῶν οὔτε ἐκ τῆς πορνείας αὐτῶν οὔτε ἐκ τῶν κλεμμάτων αὐτῶν.}

					% \item[BSV]

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Revelation 10} % *REVELATIONNT-10 
	% -----
		\label{REVELATIONNT-10}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 10:1} % *REVELATIONNT-10-1 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-10-1}

			AND I saw another mighty angel come down from heaven, clothed with a cloud: and a rainbow was upon his head, and his face was as it were the sun, and his feet as pillars of fire:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ εἶδον ἄλλον ἄγγελον ἰσχυρὸν καταβαίνοντα ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ, περιβεβλημένον νεφέλην, καὶ ἡ ἶρις ἐπὶ τὴν κεφαλὴν αὐτοῦ, καὶ τὸ πρόσωπον αὐτοῦ ὡς ὁ ἥλιος, καὶ οἱ πόδες αὐτοῦ ὡς στῦλοι πυρός,}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 10:2} % *REVELATIONNT-10-2 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-10-2}

			And he had in his hand a little book%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					See \hyperref[DC77-14]{DC 77:14}.
					}
			open: and he set his right foot upon the sea, and his left foot on the earth,

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἔχων ἐν τῇ χειρὶ αὐτοῦ βιβλαρίδιον ἠνεῳγμένον. καὶ ἔθηκεν τὸν πόδα αὐτοῦ τὸν δεξιὸν ἐπὶ τῆς θαλάσσης, τὸν δὲ εὐώνυμον ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς,}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 10:3} % *REVELATIONNT-10-3 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-10-3}

			And cried with a loud voice, as when a lion roareth: and when he had cried, seven thunders uttered their voices.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἔκραξεν φωνῇ μεγάλῃ ὥσπερ λέων μυκᾶται. καὶ ὅτε ἔκραξεν, ἐλάλησαν αἱ ἑπτὰ βρονταὶ τὰς ἑαυτῶν φωνάς.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 10:4} % *REVELATIONNT-10-4 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-10-4}

			And when the seven thunders had uttered their voices, I was about to write: and I heard a voice from heaven saying unto me, Seal up those things which the seven thunders uttered, and write them not.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ὅτε ἐλάλησαν αἱ ἑπτὰ βρονταί, ἤμελλον γράφειν· καὶ ἤκουσα φωνὴν ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ λέγουσαν· Σφράγισον ἃ ἐλάλησαν αἱ ἑπτὰ βρονταί, καὶ μὴ αὐτὰ γράψῃς.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 10:5} % *REVELATIONNT-10-5 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-10-5}

			And the angel which I saw stand upon the sea and upon the earth lifted up his hand to heaven,

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ὁ ἄγγελος, ὃν εἶδον ἑστῶτα ἐπὶ τῆς θαλάσσης καὶ ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς, ἦρεν τὴν χεῖρα αὐτοῦ τὴν δεξιὰν εἰς τὸν οὐρανόν,}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 10:6} % *REVELATIONNT-10-6 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-10-6}

			And sware by him that liveth for ever and ever, who created heaven, and the things that therein are, and the earth, and the things that therein are, and the sea, and the things which are therein, that there should be time no longer:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ὤμοσεν τῷ ζῶντι εἰς τοὺς αἰῶνας τῶν αἰώνων, ὃς ἔκτισεν τὸν οὐρανὸν καὶ τὰ ἐν αὐτῷ καὶ τὴν γῆν καὶ τὰ ἐν αὐτῇ καὶ τὴν θάλασσαν καὶ τὰ ἐν αὐτῇ, ὅτι χρόνος οὐκέτι ἔσται·}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 10:7} % *REVELATIONNT-10-7 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-10-7}

			But in the days of the voice of the seventh angel, when he shall begin to sound, the mystery of God should be finished, as he hath declared to his servants the prophets.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀλλ᾽ ἐν ταῖς ἡμέραις τῆς φωνῆς τοῦ ἑβδόμου ἀγγέλου, ὅταν μέλλῃ σαλπίζειν, καὶ ἐτελέσθη τὸ μυστήριον τοῦ θεοῦ, ὡς εὐηγγέλισεν τοὺς ἑαυτοῦ δούλους τοὺς προφήτας.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 10:8} % *REVELATIONNT-10-8 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-10-8}

			And the voice which I heard from heaven spake unto me again, and said, Go and take the little book which is open in the hand of the angel which standeth upon the sea and upon the earth.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ ἡ φωνὴ ἣν ἤκουσα ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ, πάλιν λαλοῦσαν μετ᾽ ἐμοῦ καὶ λέγουσαν· Ὕπαγε λάβε τὸ βιβλίον τὸ ἠνεῳγμένον ἐν τῇ χειρὶ τοῦ ἀγγέλου τοῦ ἑστῶτος ἐπὶ τῆς θαλάσσης καὶ ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 10:9} % *REVELATIONNT-10-9 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-10-9}

			And I went unto the angel, and said unto him, Give me the little book.%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					See \hyperref[DC77-14]{DC 77:14}.
					}
			And he said unto me, Take it, and eat it up; and it shall make thy belly bitter, but it shall be in thy mouth sweet as honey.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἀπῆλθα πρὸς τὸν ἄγγελον λέγων αὐτῷ δοῦναί μοι τὸ βιβλαρίδιον. καὶ λέγει μοι· Λάβε καὶ κατάφαγε αὐτό, καὶ πικρανεῖ σου τὴν κοιλίαν, ἀλλ᾽ ἐν τῷ στόματί σου ἔσται γλυκὺ ὡς μέλι.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]

					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 10:10} % *REVELATIONNT-10-10 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-10-10}

			And I took the little book out of the angel's hand, and ate it up; and it was in my mouth sweet as honey: and as soon as I had eaten it, my belly was bitter.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἔλαβον τὸ βιβλαρίδιον ἐκ τῆς χειρὸς τοῦ ἀγγέλου καὶ κατέφαγον αὐτό, καὶ ἦν ἐν τῷ στόματί μου ὡς μέλι γλυκύ· καὶ ὅτε ἔφαγον αὐτό, ἐπικράνθη ἡ κοιλία μου.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]

				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 10:11} % *REVELATIONNT-10-11 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-10-11}

			And he said unto me, Thou must prophesy again before many peoples, and nations, and tongues, and kings.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ λέγουσίν μοι· Δεῖ σε πάλιν προφητεῦσαι ἐπὶ λαοῖς καὶ ἔθνεσιν καὶ γλώσσαις καὶ βασιλεῦσιν πολλοῖς.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Revelation 11} % *REVELATIONNT-11 
	% -----
		\label{REVELATIONNT-11}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:1} % *REVELATIONNT-11-1 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-1}

			AND there was given me a reed like unto a rod: and the angel stood, saying, Rise, and measure the temple of God, and the altar, and them that worship therein.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ ἐδόθη μοι κάλαμος ὅμοιος ῥάβδῳ, λέγων· Ἔγειρε καὶ μέτρησον τὸν ναὸν τοῦ θεοῦ καὶ τὸ θυσιαστήριον καὶ τοὺς προσκυνοῦντας ἐν αὐτῷ.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:2} % *REVELATIONNT-11-2 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-2}

			But the court which is without the temple leave out, and measure it not; for it is given unto the Gentiles: and the holy city shall they tread under foot forty and two months.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ τὴν αὐλὴν τὴν ἔξωθεν τοῦ ναοῦ ἔκβαλε ἔξωθεν, καὶ μὴ αὐτὴν μετρήσῃς, ὅτι ἐδόθη τοῖς ἔθνεσιν, καὶ τὴν πόλιν τὴν ἁγίαν πατήσουσιν μῆνας τεσσεράκοντα δύο.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:3} % *REVELATIONNT-11-3 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-3}

			And I will give power unto my two witnesses,%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					See \hyperref[DC77-15]{DC 77:15}.
					}
			and they shall prophesy a thousand two hundred and threescore days, clothed in sackcloth.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ δώσω τοῖς δυσὶν μάρτυσίν μου, καὶ προφητεύσουσιν ἡμέρας χιλίας διακοσίας ἑξήκοντα, περιβεβλημένοι σάκκους.}

					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:4} % *REVELATIONNT-11-4 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-4}

			These are the two olive trees, and the two candlesticks standing before the God of the earth.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Οὗτοί εἰσιν αἱ δύο ἐλαῖαι καὶ αἱ δύο λυχνίαι αἱ ἐνώπιον τοῦ κυρίου τῆς γῆς ἑστῶτες.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:5} % *REVELATIONNT-11-5 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-5}

			And if any man will hurt them, fire proceedeth out of their mouth, and devoureth their enemies: and if any man will hurt them, he must in this manner be killed.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ εἴ τις αὐτοὺς θέλει ἀδικῆσαι, πῦρ ἐκπορεύεται ἐκ τοῦ στόματος αὐτῶν καὶ κατεσθίει τοὺς ἐχθροὺς αὐτῶν· καὶ εἴ τις θελήσῃ αὐτοὺς ἀδικῆσαι, οὕτως δεῖ αὐτὸν ἀποκτανθῆναι.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:6} % *REVELATIONNT-11-6 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-6}

			And if any man will hurt them, fire proceedeth out of their mouth, and devoureth their enemies: and if any man will hurt them, he must in this manner be killed.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὗτοι ἔχουσιν τὴν ἐξουσίαν κλεῖσαι τὸν οὐρανόν, ἵνα μὴ ὑετὸς βρέχῃ τὰς ἡμέρας τῆς προφητείας αὐτῶν, καὶ ἐξουσίαν ἔχουσιν ἐπὶ τῶν ὑδάτων στρέφειν αὐτὰ εἰς αἷμα καὶ πατάξαι τὴν γῆν ἐν πάσῃ πληγῇ ὁσάκις ἐὰν θελήσωσιν.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:7} % *REVELATIONNT-11-7 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-7}

			And when they shall have finished their testimony, the beast that ascendeth out of the bottomless pit shall make war against them, and shall overcome them, and kill them.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ ὅταν τελέσωσιν τὴν μαρτυρίαν αὐτῶν, τὸ θηρίον τὸ ἀναβαῖνον ἐκ τῆς ἀβύσσου ποιήσει μετ᾽ αὐτῶν πόλεμον καὶ νικήσει αὐτοὺς καὶ ἀποκτενεῖ αὐτούς.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:8} % *REVELATIONNT-11-8 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-8}

			And their dead bodies shall lie in the street of the great city, which spiritually is called Sodom and Egypt, where also our Lord was crucified.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ τὸ πτῶμα αὐτῶν ἐπὶ τῆς πλατείας τῆς πόλεως τῆς μεγάλης, ἥτις καλεῖται πνευματικῶς Σόδομα καὶ Αἴγυπτος, ὅπου καὶ ὁ κύριος αὐτῶν ἐσταυρώθη.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]

				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:9} % *REVELATIONNT-11-9 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-9}

			And they of the people and kindreds and tongues and nations shall see their dead bodies three days and an half, and shall not suffer their dead bodies to be put in graves.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ βλέπουσιν ἐκ τῶν λαῶν καὶ φυλῶν καὶ γλωσσῶν καὶ ἐθνῶν τὸ πτῶμα αὐτῶν ἡμέρας τρεῖς καὶ ἥμισυ, καὶ τὰ πτώματα αὐτῶν οὐκ ἀφίουσιν τεθῆναι εἰς μνῆμα.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:10} % *REVELATIONNT-11-10 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-10}

			And they that dwell upon the earth shall rejoice over them, and make merry, and shall send gifts one to another; because these two prophets tormented them that dwelt on the earth.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ οἱ κατοικοῦντες ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς χαίρουσιν ἐπ᾽ αὐτοῖς καὶ εὐφραίνονται, καὶ δῶρα πέμψουσιν ἀλλήλοις, ὅτι οὗτοι οἱ δύο προφῆται ἐβασάνισαν τοὺς κατοικοῦντας ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:11} % *REVELATIONNT-11-11 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-11}

			And after three days and an half the Spirit of life from God entered into them, and they stood upon their feet; and great fear fell upon them which saw them.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ μετὰ τὰς τρεῖς ἡμέρας καὶ ἥμισυ πνεῦμα ζωῆς ἐκ τοῦ θεοῦ εἰσῆλθεν ἐν αὐτοῖς, καὶ ἔστησαν ἐπὶ τοὺς πόδας αὐτῶν, καὶ φόβος μέγας ἐπέπεσεν ἐπὶ τοὺς θεωροῦντας αὐτούς·}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:12} % *REVELATIONNT-11-12 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-12}

			And they heard a great voice from heaven saying unto them, Come up hither. And they ascended up to heaven in a cloud; and their enemies beheld them.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἤκουσαν φωνῆς μεγάλης ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ λεγούσης αὐτοῖς· Ἀνάβατε ὧδε, καὶ ἀνέβησαν εἰς τὸν οὐρανὸν ἐν τῇ νεφέλῃ, καὶ ἐθεώρησαν αὐτοὺς οἱ ἐχθροὶ αὐτῶν.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:13} % *REVELATIONNT-11-13 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-13}

			And the same hour was there a great earthquake, and the tenth part of the city fell, and in the earthquake were slain of men seven thousand: and the remnant were affrighted, and gave glory to the God of heaven.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἐν ἐκείνῃ τῇ ὥρᾳ ἐγένετο σεισμὸς μέγας, καὶ τὸ δέκατον τῆς πόλεως ἔπεσεν, καὶ ἀπεκτάνθησαν ἐν τῷ σεισμῷ ὀνόματα ἀνθρώπων χιλιάδες ἑπτά, καὶ οἱ λοιποὶ ἔμφοβοι ἐγένοντο καὶ ἔδωκαν δόξαν τῷ θεῷ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:14} % *REVELATIONNT-11-14 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-14}

			The second woe is past; and, behold, the third woe cometh quickly.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἡ οὐαὶ ἡ δευτέρα ἀπῆλθεν· ἰδοὺ ἡ οὐαὶ ἡ τρίτη ἔρχεται ταχύ.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:15} % *REVELATIONNT-11-15 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-15}

			And the seventh angel sounded; and there were great voices in heaven, saying, The kingdoms of this world are become the kingdoms of our Lord, and of his Christ; and he shall reign for ever and ever.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ ὁ ἕβδομος ἄγγελος ἐσάλπισεν· καὶ ἐγένοντο φωναὶ μεγάλαι ἐν τῷ οὐρανῷ λέγοντες· Ἐγένετο ἡ βασιλεία τοῦ κόσμου τοῦ κυρίου ἡμῶν καὶ τοῦ χριστοῦ αὐτοῦ, καὶ βασιλεύσει εἰς τοὺς αἰῶνας τῶν αἰώνων.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:16} % *REVELATIONNT-11-16 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-16}

			And the four and twenty elders,%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					See \hyperref[DC77-5]{DC 77:5}.
					}
			which sat before God on their seats, fell upon their faces, and worshipped God,

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ οἱ εἴκοσι τέσσαρες πρεσβύτεροι οἱ ἐνώπιον τοῦ θεοῦ καθήμενοι ἐπὶ τοὺς θρόνους αὐτῶν ἔπεσαν ἐπὶ τὰ πρόσωπα αὐτῶν καὶ προσεκύνησαν τῷ θεῷ,}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:17} % *REVELATIONNT-11-17 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-17}

			Saying, We give thee thanks, O Lord God Almighty, which art, and wast, and art to come; because thou hast taken to thee thy great power, and hast reigned.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{λέγοντες· Εὐχαριστοῦμέν σοι, κύριε, ὁ θεός, ὁ παντοκράτωρ, ὁ ὢν καὶ ὁ ἦν, ὅτι εἴληφας τὴν δύναμίν σου τὴν μεγάλην καὶ ἐβασίλευσας.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:18} % *REVELATIONNT-11-18 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-18}

			And the nations were angry, and thy wrath is come, and the time of the dead, that they should be judged, and that thou shouldest give reward unto thy servants the prophets, and to the saints, and them that fear thy name, small and great; and shouldest destroy them which destroy the earth.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ τὰ ἔθνη ὠργίσθησαν, καὶ ἦλθεν ἡ ὀργή σου καὶ ὁ καιρὸς τῶν νεκρῶν κριθῆναι καὶ δοῦναι τὸν μισθὸν τοῖς δούλοις σου τοῖς προφήταις καὶ τοῖς ἁγίοις καὶ τοῖς φοβουμένοις τὸ ὄνομά σου, τοὺς μικροὺς καὶ τοὺς μεγάλους, καὶ διαφθεῖραι τοὺς διαφθείροντας τὴν γῆν.}

			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 11:19} % *REVELATIONNT-11-19 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-11-19}

			And the temple of God was opened in heaven, and there was seen in his temple the ark of his testament: and there were lightnings, and voices, and thunderings, and an earthquake, and great hail.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ ἠνοίγη ὁ ναὸς τοῦ θεοῦ ὁ ἐν τῷ οὐρανῷ, καὶ ὤφθη ἡ κιβωτὸς τῆς διαθήκης αὐτοῦ ἐν τῷ ναῷ αὐτοῦ· καὶ ἐγένοντο ἀστραπαὶ καὶ φωναὶ καὶ βρονταὶ καὶ σεισμὸς καὶ χάλαζα μεγάλη.}

					% \item[BSV]

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Revelation 12} % *REVELATIONNT-12 
	% -----
		\label{REVELATIONNT-12}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 12:1} % *REVELATIONNT-12-1 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-12-1}

			AND there appeared a great wonder in heaven; a woman clothed with the sun, and the moon under her feet, and upon her head a crown of twelve stars:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ σημεῖον μέγα ὤφθη ἐν τῷ οὐρανῷ, γυνὴ περιβεβλημένη τὸν ἥλιον, καὶ ἡ σελήνη ὑποκάτω τῶν ποδῶν αὐτῆς, καὶ ἐπὶ τῆς κεφαλῆς αὐτῆς στέφανος ἀστέρων δώδεκα,}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]

					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 12:2} % *REVELATIONNT-12-2 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-12-2}

			And she being with child cried, travailing in birth, and pained to be delivered.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἐν γαστρὶ ἔχουσα· καὶ κράζει ὠδίνουσα καὶ βασανιζομένη τεκεῖν.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]

				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 12:3} % *REVELATIONNT-12-3 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-12-3}

			And there appeared another wonder in heaven; and behold a great red dragon, having seven heads and ten horns, and seven crowns upon his heads.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ὤφθη ἄλλο σημεῖον ἐν τῷ οὐρανῷ, καὶ ἰδοὺ δράκων μέγας πυρρός, ἔχων κεφαλὰς ἑπτὰ καὶ κέρατα δέκα καὶ ἐπὶ τὰς κεφαλὰς αὐτοῦ ἑπτὰ διαδήματα,}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 12:4} % *REVELATIONNT-12-4 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-12-4}

			And his tail drew the third part%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					For ``third part,'' see note at \hyperref[DC29-36]{DC 29:36}.
					}
			of the stars of heaven, and did cast them to the earth: and the dragon stood before the woman which was ready to be delivered, for to devour her child as soon as it was born.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἡ οὐρὰ αὐτοῦ σύρει τὸ τρίτον τῶν ἀστέρων τοῦ οὐρανοῦ, καὶ ἔβαλεν αὐτοὺς εἰς τὴν γῆν. καὶ ὁ δράκων ἕστηκεν ἐνώπιον τῆς γυναικὸς τῆς μελλούσης τεκεῖν, ἵνα ὅταν τέκῃ τὸ τέκνον αὐτῆς καταφάγῃ.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
					\item[ASV] And his tail draweth the third part of the stars of heaven, and did cast them to the earth: and the dragon standeth before the woman that is about to be delivered, that when she is delivered he may devour her child.
					\item[ESV] His tail swept down a third of the stars of heaven and cast them to the earth. And the dragon stood before the woman who was about to give birth, so that when she bore her child he might devour it.
					\item[NASB] And his tail swept away a third of the stars of heaven and threw them to the earth. And the dragon stood before the woman who was about to give birth, so that when she gave birth he might devour her child.
					\item[NIRV] His tail swept a third of the stars out of the sky. It threw them down to earth. The dragon stood in front of the woman who was about to have a baby. He wanted to eat her child the moment it was born.
					\item[NIV] Its tail swept a third of the stars out of the sky and flung them to the earth. The dragon stood in front of the woman who was about to give birth, so that it might devour her child the moment he was born.
					\item[NRSV] His tail swept down a third of the stars of heaven and threw them to the earth. Then the dragon stood before the woman who was about to bear a child, so that he might devour her child as soon as it was born.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 12:5} % *REVELATIONNT-12-5 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-12-5}

			And she brought forth a man child, who was to rule all nations with a rod of iron: and her child was caught up unto God, and to his throne.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἔτεκεν υἱόν, ἄρσεν, ὃς μέλλει ποιμαίνειν πάντα τὰ ἔθνη ἐν ῥάβδῳ σιδηρᾷ· καὶ ἡρπάσθη τὸ τέκνον αὐτῆς πρὸς τὸν θεὸν καὶ πρὸς τὸν θρόνον αὐτοῦ.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 12:6} % *REVELATIONNT-12-6 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-12-6}

			And the woman fled into the wilderness, where she hath a place prepared of God, that they should feed her there a thousand two hundred and threescore days.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἡ γυνὴ ἔφυγεν εἰς τὴν ἔρημον, ὅπου ἔχει ἐκεῖ τόπον ἡτοιμασμένον ἀπὸ τοῦ θεοῦ, ἵνα ἐκεῖ τρέφωσιν αὐτὴν ἡμέρας χιλίας διακοσίας ἑξήκοντα.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 12:7} % *REVELATIONNT-12-7 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-12-7}

			And there was war in heaven: Michael and his angels fought against the dragon; and the dragon fought and his angels,

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ ἐγένετο πόλεμος ἐν τῷ οὐρανῷ, ὁ Μιχαὴλ καὶ οἱ ἄγγελοι αὐτοῦ τοῦ πολεμῆσαι μετὰ τοῦ δράκοντος. καὶ ὁ δράκων ἐπολέμησεν καὶ οἱ ἄγγελοι αὐτοῦ,}

					% \item[ASV]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---

		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-12-8}

			And prevailed not; neither was their place found any more in heaven.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ οὐκ ἴσχυσεν, οὐδὲ τόπος εὑρέθη αὐτῶν ἔτι ἐν τῷ φιλαδελφίαν}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 12:9} % *REVELATIONNT-12-9 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-12-9}

			And the great dragon was cast out, that old serpent, called the Devil, and Satan, which deceiveth the whole world: he was cast out into the earth, and his angels were cast out with him.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἐβλήθη ὁ δράκων ὁ μέγας, ὁ ὄφις ὁ ἀρχαῖος, ὁ καλούμενος Διάβολος καὶ ὁ Σατανᾶς, ὁ πλανῶν τὴν οἰκουμένην ὅλην— ἐβλήθη εἰς τὴν γῆν, καὶ οἱ ἄγγελοι αὐτοῦ μετ᾽ αὐτοῦ ἐβλήθησαν.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 12:10} % *REVELATIONNT-12-10 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-12-10}

			And I heard a loud voice saying in heaven, Now is come salvation, and strength, and the kingdom of our God, and the power of his Christ: for the accuser of our brethren is cast down, which accused them before our God day and night.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ ἤκουσα φωνὴν μεγάλην ἐν τῷ οὐρανῷ λέγουσαν· Ἄρτι ἐγένετο ἡ σωτηρία καὶ ἡ δύναμις καὶ ἡ βασιλεία τοῦ θεοῦ ἡμῶν καὶ ἡ ἐξουσία τοῦ χριστοῦ αὐτοῦ, ὅτι ἐβλήθη ὁ κατήγωρ τῶν ἀδελφῶν ἡμῶν, ὁ κατηγορῶν αὐτοὺς ἐνώπιον τοῦ θεοῦ ἡμῶν ἡμέρας καὶ νυκτός.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 12:11} % *REVELATIONNT-12-11 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-12-11}

			And they overcame him by the blood of the Lamb, and by the word of their testimony; and they loved not their lives unto the death.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ αὐτοὶ ἐνίκησαν αὐτὸν διὰ τὸ αἷμα τοῦ ἀρνίου καὶ διὰ τὸν λόγον τῆς μαρτυρίας αὐτῶν, καὶ οὐκ ἠγάπησαν τὴν ψυχὴν αὐτῶν ἄχρι θανάτου·}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]

					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 12:12} % *REVELATIONNT-12-12 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-12-12}

			Therefore rejoice, ye heavens, and ye that dwell in them. Woe to the inhabiters of the earth and of the sea! for the devil is come down unto you, having great wrath, because he knoweth that he hath but a short time.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{διὰ τοῦτο εὐφραίνεσθε, οἱ οὐρανοὶ καὶ οἱ ἐν αὐτοῖς σκηνοῦντες. οὐαὶ τὴν γῆν καὶ τὴν θάλασσαν, ὅτι κατέβη ὁ διάβολος πρὸς ὑμᾶς, ἔχων θυμὸν μέγαν, εἰδὼς ὅτι ὀλίγον καιρὸν ἔχει.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 12:13} % *REVELATIONNT-12-13 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-12-13}

			And when the dragon saw that he was cast unto the earth, he persecuted the woman which brought forth the man child.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ ὅτε εἶδεν ὁ δράκων ὅτι ἐβλήθη εἰς τὴν γῆν, ἐδίωξεν τὴν γυναῖκα ἥτις ἔτεκεν τὸν ἄρσενα.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]

					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 12:14} % *REVELATIONNT-12-14 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-12-14}

			And to the woman were given two wings of a great eagle, that she might fly into the wilderness, into her place, where she is nourished for a time, and times, and half a time, from the face of the serpent.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἐδόθησαν τῇ γυναικὶ αἱ δύο πτέρυγες τοῦ ἀετοῦ τοῦ μεγάλου, ἵνα πέτηται εἰς τὴν ἔρημον εἰς τὸν τόπον αὐτῆς, ὅπου τρέφεται ἐκεῖ καιρὸν καὶ καιροὺς καὶ ἥμισυ καιροῦ ἀπὸ προσώπου τοῦ ὄφεως.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]

					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 12:15} % *REVELATIONNT-12-15 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-12-15}

			And the serpent cast out of his mouth water as a flood after the woman, that he might cause her to be carried away of the flood.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἔβαλεν ὁ ὄφις ἐκ τοῦ στόματος αὐτοῦ ὀπίσω τῆς γυναικὸς ὕδωρ ὡς ποταμόν, ἵνα αὐτὴν ποταμοφόρητον ποιήσῃ.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 12:16} % *REVELATIONNT-12-16 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-12-16}

			And the earth helped the woman, and the earth opened her mouth, and swallowed up the flood which the dragon cast out of his mouth.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἐβοήθησεν ἡ γῆ τῇ γυναικί, καὶ ἤνοιξεν ἡ γῆ τὸ στόμα αὐτῆς καὶ κατέπιεν τὸν ποταμὸν ὃν ἔβαλεν ὁ δράκων ἐκ τοῦ στόματος αὐτοῦ·}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]

					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ὠργίσθη ὁ δράκων ἐπὶ τῇ γυναικί, καὶ ἀπῆλθεν ποιῆσαι πόλεμον μετὰ τῶν λοιπῶν τοῦ σπέρματος αὐτῆς, τῶν τηρούντων τὰς ἐντολὰς τοῦ θεοῦ καὶ ἐχόντων τὴν μαρτυρίαν Ἰησοῦ·}

	\subsubsection{Revelation 13} % *REVELATIONNT-13 
	% -----
		\label{REVELATIONNT-13}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 13:1} % *REVELATIONNT-13-1 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-13-1}

			AND I stood upon the sand of the sea, and saw a beast rise up out of the sea, having seven heads and ten horns, and upon his horns ten crowns, and upon his heads the name of blasphemy.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ εἶδον ἐκ τῆς θαλάσσης θηρίον ἀναβαῖνον, ἔχον κέρατα δέκα καὶ κεφαλὰς ἑπτά, καὶ ἐπὶ τῶν κεράτων αὐτοῦ δέκα διαδήματα, καὶ ἐπὶ τὰς κεφαλὰς αὐτοῦ ὀνόματα βλασφημίας.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]

					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 13:2} % *REVELATIONNT-13-2 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-13-2}

			And the beast which I saw was like unto a leopard, and his feet were as the feet of a bear, and his mouth as the mouth of a lion: and the dragon gave him his power, and his seat, and great authority.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ τὸ θηρίον ὃ εἶδον ἦν ὅμοιον παρδάλει, καὶ οἱ πόδες αὐτοῦ ὡς ἄρκου, καὶ τὸ στόμα αὐτοῦ ὡς στόμα λέοντος. καὶ ἔδωκεν αὐτῷ ὁ δράκων τὴν δύναμιν αὐτοῦ καὶ τὸν θρόνον αὐτοῦ καὶ ἐξουσίαν μεγάλην.}

		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-13-3}

			And I saw one of his heads as it were wounded to death; and his deadly wound was healed: and all the world wondered after the beast.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ μίαν ἐκ τῶν κεφαλῶν αὐτοῦ ὡς ἐσφαγμένην εἰς θάνατον, καὶ ἡ πληγὴ τοῦ θανάτου αὐτοῦ ἐθεραπεύθη. καὶ ἐθαυμάσθη ὅλη ἡ γῆ ὀπίσω τοῦ θηρίου,}

		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-13-4}

			And they worshipped the dragon which gave power unto the beast: and they worshipped the beast, saying, Who is like unto the beast? who is able to make war with him?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ προσεκύνησαν τῷ δράκοντι ὅτι ἔδωκεν τὴν ἐξουσίαν τῷ θηρίῳ, καὶ προσεκύνησαν τῷ θηρίῳ λέγοντες· Τίς ὅμοιος τῷ θηρίῳ, καὶ τίς δύναται πολεμῆσαι μετ᾽ αὐτοῦ;}

			And there was given unto him a mouth speaking great things and blasphemies; and power was given unto him to continue forty and two months.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ ἐδόθη αὐτῷ στόμα λαλοῦν μεγάλα καὶ βλασφημίας, καὶ ἐδόθη αὐτῷ ἐξουσία ποιῆσαι μῆνας τεσσεράκοντα δύο.}

		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-13-6}

			And he opened his mouth in blasphemy against God, to blaspheme his name, and his tabernacle, and them that dwell in heaven.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἤνοιξε τὸ στόμα αὐτοῦ εἰς βλασφημίας πρὸς τὸν θεόν, βλασφημῆσαι τὸ ὄνομα αὐτοῦ καὶ τὴν σκηνὴν αὐτοῦ, τοὺς ἐν τῷ οὐρανῷ σκηνοῦντας.}

		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-13-7}

			And it was given unto him to make war with the saints, and to overcome them: and power was given him over all kindreds, and tongues, and nations.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἐδόθη αὐτῷ ποιῆσαι πόλεμον μετὰ τῶν ἁγίων καὶ νικῆσαι αὐτούς, καὶ ἐδόθη αὐτῷ ἐξουσία ἐπὶ πᾶσαν φυλὴν καὶ λαὸν καὶ γλῶσσαν καὶ ἔθνος.}

		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-13-8}

			And all that dwell upon the earth shall worship him, whose names are not written in the book of life of the Lamb slain from the foundation of the world.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ προσκυνήσουσιν αὐτὸν πάντες οἱ κατοικοῦντες ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς, οὗ οὐ γέγραπται τὸ ὄνομα αὐτοῦ ἐν τῷ βιβλίῳ τῆς ζωῆς τοῦ ἀρνίου τοῦ ἐσφαγμένου ἀπὸ καταβολῆς κόσμου.}

		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-13-10}

			He that leadeth into captivity shall go into captivity: he that killeth with the sword must be killed with the sword. Here is the patience and the faith of the saints.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἴ τις εἰς αἰχμαλωσίαν, εἰς αἰχμαλωσίαν ὑπάγει· εἴ τις ἐν μαχαίρῃ ἀποκτανθῆναι αὐτὸν ἐν μαχαίρῃ ἀποκτανθῆναι. ὧδέ ἐστιν ἡ ὑπομονὴ καὶ ἡ πίστις τῶν ἁγίων.}

		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-13-11}

			And I beheld another beast coming up out of the earth; and he had two horns like a lamb, and he spake as a dragon.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ εἶδον ἄλλο θηρίον ἀναβαῖνον ἐκ τῆς γῆς, καὶ εἶχεν κέρατα δύο ὅμοια ἀρνίῳ, καὶ ἐλάλει ὡς δράκων.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 13:12} % *REVELATIONNT-13-12 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-13-12}

			And he exerciseth all the power of the first beast before him, and causeth the earth and them which dwell therein to worship the first beast, whose deadly wound was healed.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ τὴν ἐξουσίαν τοῦ πρώτου θηρίου πᾶσαν ποιεῖ ἐνώπιον αὐτοῦ. καὶ ποιεῖ τὴν γῆν καὶ τοὺς ἐν αὐτῇ κατοικοῦντας ἵνα προσκυνήσουσιν τὸ θηρίον τὸ πρῶτον, οὗ ἐθεραπεύθη ἡ πληγὴ τοῦ θανάτου αὐτοῦ.}

		% ---

		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-13-13}

			And he doeth great wonders, so that he maketh fire come down from heaven on the earth in the sight of men,

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ποιεῖ σημεῖα μεγάλα, ἵνα καὶ πῦρ ποιῇ ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ καταβαίνειν εἰς τὴν γῆν ἐνώπιον τῶν ἀνθρώπων.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 13:14} % *REVELATIONNT-13-14 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-13-14}

			And deceiveth them that dwell on the earth by the means of those miracles which he had power to do in the sight of the beast; saying to them that dwell on the earth, that they should make an image to the beast, which had the wound by a sword, and did live.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ πλανᾷ τοὺς κατοικοῦντας ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς διὰ τὰ σημεῖα ἃ ἐδόθη αὐτῷ ποιῆσαι ἐνώπιον τοῦ θηρίου, λέγων τοῖς κατοικοῦσιν ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς ποιῆσαι εἰκόνα τῷ θηρίῳ, ὃς ἔχει τὴν πληγὴν τῆς μαχαίρης καὶ ἔζησεν.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 13:15} % *REVELATIONNT-13-15 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-13-15}

			And he had power to give life unto the image of the beast, that the image of the beast should both speak, and cause that as many as would not worship the image of the beast should be killed.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἐδόθη αὐτῷ δοῦναι πνεῦμα τῇ εἰκόνι τοῦ θηρίου, ἵνα καὶ λαλήσῃ ἡ εἰκὼν τοῦ θηρίου καὶ ποιήσῃ ἵνα ὅσοι ἐὰν μὴ προσκυνήσωσιν τῇ εἰκόνι τοῦ θηρίου ἀποκτανθῶσιν.}

		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-13-16}

			And he causeth all, both small and great, rich and poor, free and bond, to receive a mark in their right hand, or in their foreheads:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ποιεῖ πάντας, τοὺς μικροὺς καὶ τοὺς μεγάλους, καὶ τοὺς πλουσίους καὶ τοὺς πτωχούς, καὶ τοὺς ἐλευθέρους καὶ τοὺς δούλους, ἵνα δῶσιν αὐτοῖς χάραγμα ἐπὶ τῆς χειρὸς αὐτῶν τῆς δεξιᾶς ἢ ἐπὶ τὸ μέτωπον αὐτῶν,}

		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-13-17}

			And that no man might buy or sell, save he that had the mark, or the name of the beast, or the number of his name.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἵνα μή τις δύνηται ἀγοράσαι ἢ πωλῆσαι εἰ μὴ ὁ ἔχων τὸ χάραγμα, τὸ ὄνομα τοῦ θηρίου ἢ τὸν ἀριθμὸν τοῦ ὀνόματος αὐτοῦ.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 13:18} % *REVELATIONNT-13-18 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-13-18}

			Here is wisdom. Let him that hath understanding count the number of the beast: for it is the number of a man; and his number is Six hundred threescore and six.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὧδε ἡ σοφία ἐστίν· ὁ ἔχων νοῦν ψηφισάτω τὸν ἀριθμὸν τοῦ θηρίου, ἀριθμὸς γὰρ ἀνθρώπου ἐστίν· καὶ ὁ ἀριθμὸς αὐτοῦ ἑξακόσιοι ἑξήκοντα ἕξ.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

				\end{compactdesc}

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Revelation 14} % *REVELATIONNT-14 
	% -----
		\label{REVELATIONNT-14}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 14:1} % *REVELATIONNT-14-1 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-1}

			AND I looked, and, lo, a Lamb stood on the mount Sion, and with him an hundred forty and four thousand, having his Father's name written in their foreheads.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ εἶδον, καὶ ἰδοὺ τὸ ἀρνίον ἑστὸς ἐπὶ τὸ ὄρος Σιών, καὶ μετ᾽ αὐτοῦ ἑκατὸν τεσσεράκοντα τέσσαρες χιλιάδες ἔχουσαι τὸ ὄνομα αὐτοῦ καὶ τὸ ὄνομα τοῦ πατρὸς αὐτοῦ γεγραμμένον ἐπὶ τῶν μετώπων αὐτῶν.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 14:2} % *REVELATIONNT-14-2 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-2}

			And I heard a voice from heaven, as the voice of many waters, and as the voice of a great thunder: and I heard the voice of harpers harping with their harps:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἤκουσα φωνὴν ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ ὡς φωνὴν ὑδάτων πολλῶν καὶ ὡς φωνὴν βροντῆς μεγάλης, καὶ ἡ φωνὴ ἣν ἤκουσα ὡς κιθαρῳδῶν κιθαριζόντων ἐν ταῖς κιθάραις αὐτῶν.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 14:3} % *REVELATIONNT-14-3 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-3}

			And they sung as it were a new song before the throne, and before the four beasts, and the elders: and no man could learn that song but the hundred and forty and four thousand, which were redeemed from the earth.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ᾄδουσιν ὡς ᾠδὴν καινὴν ἐνώπιον τοῦ θρόνου καὶ ἐνώπιον τῶν τεσσάρων ζῴων καὶ τῶν πρεσβυτέρων· καὶ οὐδεὶς ἐδύνατο μαθεῖν τὴν ᾠδὴν εἰ μὴ αἱ ἑκατὸν τεσσεράκοντα τέσσαρες χιλιάδες, οἱ ἠγορασμένοι ἀπὸ τῆς γῆς.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 14:4} % *REVELATIONNT-14-4 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-4}

			These are they which were not defiled with women; for they are virgins. These are they which follow the Lamb whithersoever he goeth. These were redeemed from among men, being the firstfruits unto God and to the Lamb.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὗτοί εἰσιν οἳ μετὰ γυναικῶν οὐκ ἐμολύνθησαν, παρθένοι γάρ εἰσιν· οὗτοι οἱ ἀκολουθοῦντες τῷ ἀρνίῳ ὅπου ἂν ὑπάγῃ· οὗτοι ἠγοράσθησαν ἀπὸ τῶν ἀνθρώπων ἀπαρχὴ τῷ θεῷ καὶ τῷ ἀρνίῳ,}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

			\end{framed}

		% ---

		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-5}

			And in their mouth was found no guile: for they are without fault before the throne of God.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἐν τῷ στόματι αὐτῶν οὐχ εὑρέθη ψεῦδος· ἄμωμοί εἰσιν.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 14:6} % *REVELATIONNT-14-6 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-6}

			And I saw another angel fly in the midst of heaven, having the everlasting gospel to preach unto them that dwell on the earth, and to every nation, and kindred, and tongue, and people,

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ εἶδον ἄλλον ἄγγελον πετόμενον ἐν μεσουρανήματι, ἔχοντα εὐαγγέλιον αἰώνιον εὐαγγελίσαι ἐπὶ τοὺς καθημένους ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς καὶ ἐπὶ πᾶν ἔθνος καὶ φυλὴν καὶ γλῶσσαν καὶ λαόν,}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 14:7} % *REVELATIONNT-14-7 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-7}

			Saying with a loud voice, Fear God, and give glory to him; for the hour of his judgment is come: and worship him that made heaven, and earth, and the sea, and the fountains of waters.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{λέγων ἐν φωνῇ μεγάλῃ· Φοβήθητε τὸν θεὸν καὶ δότε αὐτῷ δόξαν, ὅτι ἦλθεν ἡ ὥρα τῆς κρίσεως αὐτοῦ, καὶ προσκυνήσατε τῷ ποιήσαντι τὸν οὐρανὸν καὶ τὴν γῆν καὶ θάλασσαν καὶ πηγὰς ὑδάτων.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 14:8} % *REVELATIONNT-14-8 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-8}

			And there followed another angel, saying, Babylon is fallen, is fallen, that great city, because she made all nations drink of the wine of the wrath of her fornication.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ ἄλλος δεύτερος ἄγγελος ἠκολούθησεν λέγων· Ἔπεσεν, ἔπεσεν Βαβυλὼν ἡ μεγάλη, ἣ ἐκ τοῦ οἴνου τοῦ θυμοῦ τῆς πορνείας αὐτῆς πεπότικεν πάντα τὰ ἔθνη.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 14:9} % *REVELATIONNT-14-9 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-9}

			And the third angel followed them, saying with a loud voice, If any man worship the beast and his image, and receive his mark in his forehead, or in his hand,

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ ἄλλος ἄγγελος τρίτος ἠκολούθησεν αὐτοῖς λέγων ἐν φωνῇ μεγάλῃ· Εἴ τις προσκυνεῖ τὸ θηρίον καὶ τὴν εἰκόνα αὐτοῦ, καὶ λαμβάνει χάραγμα ἐπὶ τοῦ μετώπου αὐτοῦ ἢ ἐπὶ τὴν χεῖρα αὐτοῦ,}

					% \item[ASV]

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 14:10} % *REVELATIONNT-14-10 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-10}

			The same shall drink of the wine of the wrath of God, which is poured out without mixture into the cup of his indignation; and he shall be tormented with fire and brimstone in the presence of the holy angels, and in the presence of the Lamb:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ αὐτὸς πίεται ἐκ τοῦ οἴνου τοῦ θυμοῦ τοῦ θεοῦ τοῦ κεκερασμένου ἀκράτου ἐν τῷ ποτηρίῳ τῆς ὀργῆς αὐτοῦ, καὶ βασανισθήσεται ἐν πυρὶ καὶ θείῳ ἐνώπιον ἀγγέλων ἁγίων καὶ ἐνώπιον τοῦ ἀρνίου.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]

					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 14:11} % *REVELATIONNT-14-11 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-11}

			And the smoke of their torment ascendeth up for ever and ever: and they have no rest day nor night, who worship the beast and his image, and whosoever receiveth the mark of his name.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ὁ καπνὸς τοῦ βασανισμοῦ αὐτῶν εἰς αἰῶνας αἰώνων ἀναβαίνει, καὶ οὐκ ἔχουσιν ἀνάπαυσιν ἡμέρας καὶ νυκτός, οἱ προσκυνοῦντες τὸ θηρίον καὶ τὴν εἰκόνα αὐτοῦ, καὶ εἴ τις λαμβάνει τὸ χάραγμα τοῦ ὀνόματος αὐτοῦ.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 14:12} % *REVELATIONNT-14-12 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-12}

			Here is the patience of the saints: here are they that keep the commandments of God, and the faith of Jesus.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ὧδε ἡ ὑπομονὴ τῶν ἁγίων ἐστίν, οἱ τηροῦντες τὰς ἐντολὰς τοῦ θεοῦ καὶ τὴν πίστιν Ἰησοῦ.}

			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 14:13} % *REVELATIONNT-14-13 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-13}

			And I heard a voice from heaven saying unto me, Write, Blessed are the dead which die in the Lord from henceforth: Yea, saith the Spirit, that they may rest from their labours; and their works do follow them.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ ἤκουσα φωνῆς ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ λεγούσης· Γράψον· Μακάριοι οἱ νεκροὶ οἱ ἐν κυρίῳ ἀποθνῄσκοντες ἀπ᾽ ἄρτι. ναί, λέγει τὸ πνεῦμα, ἵνα ἀναπαήσονται ἐκ τῶν κόπων αὐτῶν, τὰ γὰρ ἔργα αὐτῶν ἀκολουθεῖ μετ᾽ αὐτῶν.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]

					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 14:14} % *REVELATIONNT-14-14 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-14}

			And I looked, and behold a white cloud, and upon the cloud one sat like unto the Son of man, having on his head a golden crown, and in his hand a sharp sickle.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ εἶδον, καὶ ἰδοὺ νεφέλη λευκή, καὶ ἐπὶ τὴν νεφέλην καθήμενον ὅμοιον υἱὸν ἀνθρώπου, ἔχων ἐπὶ τῆς κεφαλῆς αὐτοῦ στέφανον χρυσοῦν καὶ ἐν τῇ χειρὶ αὐτοῦ δρέπανον ὀξύ.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 14:15} % *REVELATIONNT-14-15 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-15}

			And another angel came out of the temple, crying with a loud voice to him that sat on the cloud, Thrust in thy sickle, and reap: for the time is come for thee to reap; for the harvest of the earth is ripe.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἄλλος ἄγγελος ἐξῆλθεν ἐκ τοῦ ναοῦ κράζων ἐν φωνῇ μεγάλῃ τῷ καθημένῳ ἐπὶ τῆς νεφέλης· Πέμψον τὸ δρέπανόν σου καὶ θέρισον, ὅτι ἦλθεν ἡ ὥρα θερίσαι, ὅτι ἐξηράνθη ὁ θερισμὸς τῆς γῆς.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 14:16} % *REVELATIONNT-14-16 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-16}

			And he that sat on the cloud thrust in his sickle on the earth; and the earth was reaped.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἔβαλεν ὁ καθήμενος ἐπὶ τῆς νεφέλης τὸ δρέπανον αὐτοῦ ἐπὶ τὴν γῆν, καὶ ἐθερίσθη ἡ γῆ.}

					% \item[BSV]

				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 14:17} % *REVELATIONNT-14-17 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-17}

			And another angel came out of the temple which is in heaven, he also having a sharp sickle.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ ἄλλος ἄγγελος ἐξῆλθεν ἐκ τοῦ ναοῦ τοῦ ἐν τῷ οὐρανῷ, ἔχων καὶ αὐτὸς δρέπανον ὀξύ.}

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 14:18} % *REVELATIONNT-14-18 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-18}

			And another angel came out from the altar, which had power over fire; and cried with a loud cry to him that had the sharp sickle, saying, Thrust in thy sharp sickle, and gather the clusters of the vine of the earth; for her grapes are fully ripe.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἄλλος ἄγγελος ἐκ τοῦ θυσιαστηρίου, ὁ ἔχων ἐξουσίαν ἐπὶ τοῦ πυρός, καὶ ἐφώνησεν φωνῇ μεγάλῃ τῷ ἔχοντι τὸ δρέπανον τὸ ὀξὺ λέγων· Πέμψον σου τὸ δρέπανον τὸ ὀξὺ καὶ τρύγησον τοὺς βότρυας τῆς ἀμπέλου τῆς γῆς, ὅτι ἤκμασαν αἱ σταφυλαὶ αὐτῆς.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 14:19} % *REVELATIONNT-14-19 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-14-19}

			And the angel thrust in his sickle into the earth, and gathered the vine of the earth, and cast it into the great winepress of the wrath of God.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἔβαλεν ὁ ἄγγελος τὸ δρέπανον αὐτοῦ εἰς τὴν γῆν, καὶ ἐτρύγησεν τὴν ἄμπελον τῆς γῆς, καὶ ἔβαλεν εἰς τὴν ληνὸν τοῦ θυμοῦ τοῦ θεοῦ τὸν μέγαν.}

					% \item[BSV]

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 15:1} % *REVELATIONNT-15-1 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-15-1}

			AND I saw another sign in heaven, great and marvellous,%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					This is the most important NT location for the common restoration phrase ``great and marvelous.'' See more in my note at \hyperref[DC76-2]{DC 76:2}.
					}
			seven angels having the seven last plagues; for in them is filled up the wrath of God.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ εἶδον ἄλλο σημεῖον ἐν τῷ οὐρανῷ μέγα καὶ θαυμαστόν, ἀγγέλους ἑπτὰ ἔχοντας πληγὰς ἑπτὰ τὰς ἐσχάτας, ὅτι ἐν αὐταῖς ἐτελέσθη ὁ θυμὸς τοῦ θεοῦ.}

		\paragraph{Revelation 15:2} % *REVELATIONNT-15-2 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-15-2}

			And I saw as it were a sea of glass mingled with fire: and them that had gotten the victory over the beast, and over his image, and over his mark, and over the number of his name, stand on the sea of glass, having the harps of God.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ εἶδον ὡς θάλασσαν ὑαλίνην μεμιγμένην πυρί, καὶ τοὺς νικῶντας ἐκ τοῦ θηρίου καὶ ἐκ τῆς εἰκόνος αὐτοῦ καὶ ἐκ τοῦ ἀριθμοῦ τοῦ ὀνόματος αὐτοῦ ἑστῶτας ἐπὶ τὴν θάλασσαν τὴν ὑαλίνην, ἔχοντας κιθάρας τοῦ θεοῦ.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 15:3} % *REVELATIONNT-15-3 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-15-3}

			And they sing the song of Moses the servant of God, and the song of the Lamb, saying, Great and marvellous are thy works, Lord God Almighty; just and true are thy ways, thou King of saints.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ᾄδουσιν τὴν ᾠδὴν Μωϋσέως τοῦ δούλου τοῦ θεοῦ καὶ τὴν ᾠδὴν τοῦ ἀρνίου λέγοντες· Μεγάλα καὶ θαυμαστὰ τὰ ἔργα σου, κύριε, ὁ θεός, ὁ παντοκράτωρ· δίκαιαι καὶ ἀληθιναὶ αἱ ὁδοί σου, ὁ βασιλεὺς τῶν αἰώνων·}
					\item[BSV] et cantant canticum Mosi servi Dei
									et canticum agni dicentes
									magna et mirabilia opera tua Domine Deus omnipotens
									iustae et verae viae tuae rex saeculorum
					\item[AB-1975 252] And they sang the song of Moses, the servant of God, and the song of the Lamb; they said:
						``Great and marvelous are Your works,
						Lord God Almighty;
						just and true are Your ways,
						King of Nations.
					\item[AB-2014 631] They sing the song of Moses, the servant of God, that is, the song of the Lamb,\footnotemark{}
					saying,
						``Great and awe-inspiring are your works, Lord God Almighty.
						Just and true are your ways, King of the nations.
					\item[ASV] And they sing the song of Moses the servant of God, and the song of the Lamb, saying, Great and marvellous are thy works, O Lord God, the Almighty; righteous and true are thy ways, thou King of the ages.
					\item[ESV] And they sing the song of Moses, the servant of God, and the song of the Lamb, saying, ``Great and amazing are your deeds, O Lord God the Almighty! Just and true are your ways, O King of the nations!
					\item[NASB] And they sang the song of Moses, the bond-servant of God, and the song of the Lamb, saying, ``Great and marvelous are Your works, O Lord God, the Almighty; Righteous and true are Your ways, King of the nations!
					\item[NIV] and sang the song of God's servant Moses and of the Lamb: ``Great and marvelous are your deeds, Lord God Almighty. Just and true are your ways, King of the nations.
					\item[NRSV] And they sing the song of Moses, the servant of God, and the song of the Lamb: ``Great and amazing are your deeds, Lord God the Almighty! Just and true are your ways, King of the nations!
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}
				\footnotetext{%
					\label{REVELATION-15-3-AB2014}%
					Note that this translation makes the song of Moses the same as the song of the Lamb, by using ``that is'' to draw an equivalence. It is possible to see the Greek that way, taking the \gr{καὶ} as ``even'': ``And they sing the song of Moses the servant of God, even the song of the Lamb.'' The other translations don't give the impression that the songs are one and the same, though the NIV can be read that way a bit more easily. AB-2014 comments at 632: ``Some interpreters suggest that the redeemed sing two different songs: the song of Moses from \hyperref[EXODUS-15]{Exodus 15}, which is not quoted, and the song of the Lamb, which is quoted in \hyperref[REVELATIONNT-15-3]{Revelation 15:3--4}. Most, however, connect Moses and the Lamb to the one song that is quoted. The \gr{καὶ} is explanatory (`indeed'), redefining the song of Moses as the one sung by those redeemed through the Lamb.'' 
					}

		% ---

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 17:1} % *REVELATIONNT-17-1 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-17-1}

			AND there came one of the seven angels which had the seven vials, and talked with me, saying unto me, Come hither; I will shew unto thee the judgment of the great whore that sitteth upon many waters:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ ἦλθεν εἷς ἐκ τῶν ἑπτὰ ἀγγέλων τῶν ἐχόντων τὰς ἑπτὰ φιάλας, καὶ ἐλάλησεν μετ᾽ ἐμοῦ λέγων· Δεῦρο, δείξω σοι τὸ κρίμα τῆς πόρνης τῆς μεγάλης τῆς καθημένης ἐπὶ ὑδάτων πολλῶν,}

			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 17:2} % *REVELATIONNT-17-2 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-17-2}

			With whom the kings of the earth have committed fornication, and the inhabitants of the earth have been made drunk with the wine of her fornication.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{μεθ᾽ ἧς ἐπόρνευσαν οἱ βασιλεῖς τῆς γῆς, καὶ ἐμεθύσθησαν οἱ κατοικοῦντες τὴν γῆν ἐκ τοῦ οἴνου τῆς πορνείας αὐτῆς.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 17:3} % *REVELATIONNT-17-3 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-17-3}

			So he carried me away in the spirit into the wilderness: and I saw a woman sit upon a scarlet coloured beast, full of names of blasphemy, having seven heads and ten horns.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἀπήνεγκέν με εἰς ἔρημον ἐν πνεύματι. καὶ εἶδον γυναῖκα καθημένην ἐπὶ θηρίον κόκκινον, γέμοντα ὀνόματα βλασφημίας, ἔχων κεφαλὰς ἑπτὰ καὶ κέρατα δέκα.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 17:4} % *REVELATIONNT-17-4 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-17-4}

			And the woman was arrayed in purple and scarlet colour, and decked with gold and precious stones and pearls, having a golden cup in her hand full of abominations and filthiness of her fornication:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἡ γυνὴ ἦν περιβεβλημένη πορφυροῦν καὶ κόκκινον, καὶ κεχρυσωμένη χρυσίῳ καὶ λίθῳ τιμίῳ καὶ μαργαρίταις, ἔχουσα ποτήριον χρυσοῦν ἐν τῇ χειρὶ αὐτῆς γέμον βδελυγμάτων καὶ τὰ ἀκάθαρτα τῆς πορνείας αὐτῆς,}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 17:5} % *REVELATIONNT-17-5 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-17-5}

			And upon her forehead was a name written, MYSTERY, BABYLON THE GREAT, THE MOTHER OF HARLOTS AND ABOMINATIONS OF THE EARTH.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἐπὶ τὸ μέτωπον αὐτῆς ὄνομα γεγραμμένον, μυστήριον, Βαβυλὼν ἡ μεγάλη, ἡ μήτηρ τῶν πορνῶν καὶ τῶν βδελυγμάτων τῆς γῆς.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXX*]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]

			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 17:6} % *REVELATIONNT-17-6 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-17-6}

			And I saw the woman drunken with the blood of the saints, and with the blood of the martyrs of Jesus: and when I saw her, I wondered with great admiration.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ εἶδον τὴν γυναῖκα μεθύουσαν ἐκ τοῦ αἵματος τῶν ἁγίων καὶ ἐκ τοῦ αἵματος τῶν μαρτύρων Ἰησοῦ.
						Καὶ ἐθαύμασα ἰδὼν αὐτὴν θαῦμα μέγα·}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 17:7} % *REVELATIONNT-17-7 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-17-7}

			And the angel said unto me, Wherefore didst thou marvel? I will tell thee the mystery of the woman, and of the beast that carrieth her, which hath the seven heads and ten horns.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ εἶπέν μοι ὁ ἄγγελος· Διὰ τί ἐθαύμασας; ἐγὼ ἐρῶ σοι τὸ μυστήριον τῆς γυναικὸς καὶ τοῦ θηρίου τοῦ βαστάζοντος αὐτήν, τοῦ ἔχοντος τὰς ἑπτὰ κεφαλὰς καὶ τὰ δέκα κέρατα·}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]

					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 17:8} % *REVELATIONNT-17-8 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-17-8}

			The beast that thou sawest was, and is not; and shall ascend out of the bottomless pit, and go into perdition: and they that dwell on the earth shall wonder, whose names were not written in the book of life from the foundation of the world, when they behold the beast that was, and is not, and yet is.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τὸ θηρίον ὃ εἶδες ἦν καὶ οὐκ ἔστιν, καὶ μέλλει ἀναβαίνειν ἐκ τῆς ἀβύσσου, καὶ εἰς ἀπώλειαν ὑπάγει· καὶ θαυμασθήσονται οἱ κατοικοῦντες ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς, ὧν οὐ γέγραπται τὸ ὄνομα ἐπὶ τὸ βιβλίον τῆς ζωῆς ἀπὸ καταβολῆς κόσμου, βλεπόντων τὸ θηρίον ὅτι ἦν καὶ οὐκ ἔστιν καὶ παρέσται.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 18:1} % *REVELATIONNT-18-1 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-18-1}

			AND after these things I saw another angel come down from heaven, having great power; and the earth was lightened with his glory.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Μετὰ ταῦτα εἶδον ἄλλον ἄγγελον καταβαίνοντα ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ, ἔχοντα ἐξουσίαν μεγάλην, καὶ ἡ γῆ ἐφωτίσθη ἐκ τῆς δόξης αὐτοῦ.}

		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-18-2}

			And he cried mightily with a strong voice, saying, Babylon the great is fallen, is fallen, and is become the habitation of devils, and the hold of every foul spirit, and a cage of every unclean and hateful bird.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἔκραξεν ἐν ἰσχυρᾷ φωνῇ λέγων· Ἔπεσεν, ἔπεσεν Βαβυλὼν ἡ μεγάλη, καὶ ἐγένετο κατοικητήριον δαιμονίων καὶ φυλακὴ παντὸς πνεύματος ἀκαθάρτου καὶ φυλακὴ παντὸς ὀρνέου ἀκαθάρτου καὶ φυλακὴ παντὸς θηρίου ἀκαθάρτου καὶ μεμισημένου,}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 18:3} % *REVELATIONNT-18-3 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-18-3}

			For all nations have drunk of the wine of the wrath of her fornication, and the kings of the earth have committed fornication with her, and the merchants of the earth are waxed rich through the abundance of her delicacies.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὅτι ἐκ τοῦ οἴνου τοῦ θυμοῦ τῆς πορνείας αὐτῆς πέπτωκαν πάντα τὰ ἔθνη, καὶ οἱ βασιλεῖς τῆς γῆς μετ᾽ αὐτῆς ἐπόρνευσαν, καὶ οἱ ἔμποροι τῆς γῆς ἐκ τῆς δυνάμεως τοῦ στρήνους αὐτῆς ἐπλούτησαν.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 18:4} % *REVELATIONNT-18-4 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-18-4}

			And I heard another voice from heaven, saying, Come out of her, my people, that ye be not partakers of her sins, and that ye receive not of her plagues.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καὶ ἤκουσα ἄλλην φωνὴν ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ λέγουσαν· Ἐξέλθατε, ὁ λαός μου, ἐξ αὐτῆς, ἵνα μὴ συγκοινωνήσητε ταῖς ἁμαρτίαις αὐτῆς, καὶ ἐκ τῶν πληγῶν αὐτῆς ἵνα μὴ λάβητε·}

		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-18-5}

			For her sins have reached unto heaven, and God hath remembered her iniquities.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὅτι ἐκολλήθησαν αὐτῆς αἱ ἁμαρτίαι ἄχρι τοῦ οὐρανοῦ, καὶ ἐμνημόνευσεν ὁ θεὸς τὰ ἀδικήματα αὐτῆς.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 18:6} % *REVELATIONNT-18-6 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-18-6}

			Reward her even as she rewarded you, and double unto her double according to her works: in the cup which she hath filled fill to her double.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀπόδοτε αὐτῇ ὡς καὶ αὐτὴ ἀπέδωκεν, καὶ διπλώσατε τὰ διπλᾶ κατὰ τὰ ἔργα αὐτῆς· ἐν τῷ ποτηρίῳ ᾧ ἐκέρασεν κεράσατε αὐτῇ διπλοῦν·}

					% \item[BSV]

		% ---
		\paragraph{Revelation 18:7} % *REVELATIONNT-18-7 
		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-18-7}

			How much she hath glorified herself, and lived deliciously, so much torment and sorrow give her: for she saith in her heart, I sit a queen, and am no widow, and shall see no sorrow.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὅσα ἐδόξασεν αὑτὴν καὶ ἐστρηνίασεν, τοσοῦτον δότε αὐτῇ βασανισμὸν καὶ πένθος. ὅτι ἐν τῇ καρδίᾳ αὐτῆς λέγει ὅτι Κάθημαι βασίλισσα, καὶ χήρα οὐκ εἰμί, καὶ πένθος οὐ μὴ ἴδω.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

			\end{framed}

		% ---
			\label{REVELATIONNT-18-8}

			Therefore shall her plagues come in one day, death, and mourning, and famine; and she shall be utterly burned with fire: for strong is the Lord God who judgeth her.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{διὰ τοῦτο ἐν μιᾷ ἡμέρᾳ ἥξουσιν αἱ πληγαὶ αὐτῆς, θάνατος καὶ πένθος καὶ λιμός, καὶ ἐν πυρὶ κατακαυθήσεται· ὅτι ἰσχυρὸς κύριος ὁ θεὸς ὁ κρίνας αὐτήν.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
